Always ask **one question at a time** via `ask_question`.\n  - For each question provide: why the decision matters, concrete options, a recommended option prefixed with `(Recommended)`, and the technical rationale.\n  - **Inspect First Rule**: Check existing codebase files before asking questions that can be answered from inspection.\n- **Handoff**: Concludes with structured `ClarificationSummary` JSON transferred to `coding-specify`.\n\n### Stage 2: Specification (`coding-specify`)\n- **Objective**: Author a comprehensive, verifiable technical specification document (`specification.md`).\n- **Contents**: Problem motivation, functional requirements, non-functional requirements, affected repositories, test contracts, and edge-case mitigations.\n- **Interactive Approval Gate**: Requires user approval via Antigravity's interactive modal before proceeding.\n\n### Stage 3: Planning (`coding-plan`)\n- **Objective**: Deconstruct the approved specification into atomic, verifiable steps and determine git branch names (`plan.md`).\n- **Multi-Repository Isolation**:\n  - Assigns work strictly per repository (respecting workspace boundaries).\n  - Branch conventions: `feature/<name>` (new capabilities), `task/<name>` (maintenance/refactoring), `bugfix/<name>` (bug fixes).\n- **Interactive Approval Gate**: Requires user approval via Antigravity's interactive modal before starting development.\n\n### Stage 4: Development (`coding-develop`)\n- **Objective**: Implement required code changes, tests, linters, documentation, and CI/CD updates.\n- **Parallel Fan-Out**: When multiple repositories are affected, `@coding` spawns independent `coding-develop` workers per repo in parallel (`coding-develop-<repo>`).\n- **Quality Mandates**:\n  - Run all unit and integration tests; verify 100% clean passes.\n  - Preserve all existing comments and docstrings unrelated to code changes.\n  - Output structured `DevelopReport` JSON summarizing modified files and test outputs.\n\n### Stage 5: Review (`coding-review`)\n- **Objective**: Validate all deliverables against the original specification and verify cross-repo contract compatibility.\n- **Discrepancy Routing**:\n  - If regressions or specification discrepancies are found: Route issues back to `coding-plan` with actionable feedback.\n  - If approved: Present validation summary to the user and request confirmation to proceed to merge.\n\n### Stage 6: Merge & Release (`coding-merge`)\n- **Objective**: Safely integrate approved changes into repository release branches.\n- **Merge Strategy**:\n  - **No Remote Repository**: Checkout base branch (`master`/`main`) and execute local `git merge <branch>`.\n  - **Remote Repository Configured**: Push branch to origin (`git push origin <branch>`) and create a Pull Request via GitHub CLI (`gh pr create`).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Specialized Workflows\n\n### 3.1 Standalone Issue Investigation (`@coding-investigate`)\nFor bugs, anomalies, and test failures, `@coding-investigate` runs an autonomous multi-repository investigation:\n1. Search code, error messages, stack traces, configs, and git history across all repositories.\n2. Formulate root cause analysis and proposed remediation in an `issue-assessment.md` artifact.\n3. Submit `issue-assessment.md` for interactive user approval (`RequestFeedback=True`).\n4. Upon user approval, confirm whether to begin automated correction; if confirmed, invoke `@coding` to execute the 6-stage lifecycle.\n\n### 3.2 Telemetry & Stream Surveillance (`@monitoring` / `@coding-monitoring`)\nFor long-running tasks, Docker logs, serial consoles (`/dev/tty*`), or continuous build streams:\n1. Stream output continuously while scanning for error patterns and stack traces.\n2. Upon anomaly detection, immediately pause surveillance and formulate a `MonitoringAlert` JSON.\n3. Prompt the user with intervention options (Restart, Ignore, Abort, Inspect code) via `ask_question`.\n\n---\n\n## 4.   "Replacement": "def parse_agent_frontmatter(md_path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract YAML frontmatter name and description from a markdown file.\"\"\"\n    content = md_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    fm_match = re.match(r\"^---\\s*\\n(.*?)\\n---\", content, re.DOTALL)\n    metadata = {}\n    if fm_match:\n        for line in fm_match.group(1).splitlines():\n            if \":\" in line:\n                key, val = line.split(\":\", 1)\n                metadata[key.strip()] = val.strip().strip('\"').strip(\"'\")\n    if \"name\" not in metadata:\n        if md_path.stem == \"agent\" and md_path.parent != AGENTS_DIR:\n            metadata[\"name\"] = md_path.parent.name\n        else:\n            metadata[\"name\"] = md_path.stem\n    return metadata\n\n\ndef define_agent(\n    name: str,\n    description: str,\n    system_prompt: str,\n    enable_write_tools: bool = True,\n    enable_subagent_tools: bool = False,\n    enable_mcp_tools: bool = True,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Define a subagent registration specification for use with define_agent / define_subagent.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"name\": name,\n        \"description\": description,\n        \"system_prompt\": system_prompt,\n        \"enable_write_tools\": enable_write_tools,\n        \"enable_subagent_tools\": enable_subagent_tools,\n        \"enable_mcp_tools\": enable_mcp_tools,\n    }\n\n\n# Alias for define_subagent compatibility\ndefine_subagent = define_agent\n\n\ndef find_agent_files() -> list[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Find canonical agent markdown files across subdirectories and top-level agents.\"\"\"\n    if not AGENTS_DIR.exists():\n        return []\n    files: list[Path] = []\n    seen_names: set[str] = set()\n\n    # 1. Coding orchestrator and standalone issue investigators require subagent tools\n        # to invoke worker subagents or handoff remediation to the coding agent.\n        is_orchestrator_or_investigator = name in (\n            \"coding\",\n            \"coding-investigate\",\n            \"coding-investigator\",\n            \"coding-invetigator\",\n            \"agent\",\n        )\n        enable_write = True\n        enable_subagents = is_orchestrator_or_investigator\n\n        agent_spec = define_agent(\n            name=name,\n            description=desc,\n            system_prompt=body,\n            enable_write_tools=enable_write,\n            enable_subagent_tools=enable_subagents,\n            enable_mcp_tools=True,\n        )\n        registered.append(agent_spec)\n\n    return registered\n\n\ndef discover_agents() -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Scan agentic-coding-plugin/agents/ for all markdown agent definitions.\"\"\"\n    agents = []\n    agent_files = find_agent_files()\n    for md_file in agent_files:\n        meta = parse_agent_frontmatter(md_file)\n        meta[\"file_path\"] = str(md_file)\n        agents.append(meta)\n    return agents",

{
  "Content": "---\nname: agent\ndescription: Architecture overview and catalog for the agentic-monitoring-plugin subagents.\n---\n\n# Monitoring Subagents & Telemetry Architecture (`agents/agent.md`)\n\nThis document defines the subagent architecture and catalog for the **`agentic-monitoring-plugin`**.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Architectural Overview\n\nThe `agentic-monitoring-plugin` provides continuous stream surveillance, log observation, and anomaly detection capabilities for Google Antigravity. By isolating monitoring into dedicated subagents, long-running processes (serial consoles, Docker containers, application logs) can be observed continuously without blocking the main conversational flow or software development pipeline.\n\n### Directory Structure\n```text\nagents/\n├── agent.md                    # Master subagent guide (this file)\n└── monitoring/                 # Telemetry & Stream Surveillance Subagent\n    └── agent.md\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Subagent Catalog\n\n| Agent Handle | Directory | `enable_write_tools` | `enable_subagent_tools` | `enable_mcp_tools` | Description |\n| :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |\n| **`@monitoring`** | [`agents/monitoring/`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/agents/monitoring/) | `True` | `False` | `True` | Background surveillance worker watching logs, serial consoles, and container streams with anomaly pause gates. |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Telemetry & Pause Gate Protocol\n\n1. **Target Attachment**:\n   - Application log files (`tail -f /path/to/log`)\n   - Container output streams (`docker logs -f <container>`)\n   - Hardware serial streams (`/dev/tty*` at configured baud rates)\n2. **Anomaly Detection**:\n   - Scans stream chunks in real-time for panics, exceptions, FATAL errors, and stack traces.\n3. **Interactive Pause Gate**:\n   - Immediately pauses stream surveillance upon anomaly discovery.\n   - Formulates a structured `MonitoringAlert` JSON summarizing timestamp, stream source, error excerpt, and severity.\n   - Uses `ask_question` to present intervention options:\n     - `(Recommended) Inspect code around stack trace`\n     - `Restart process / container`\n     - `Ignore and continue monitoring`\n     - `Abort monitoring session`\n",
  "Overwrite": true,
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/agents/agent.md"
}
> ```

{
  "Content": "# Monitoring Guidelines & Standards — agentic-monitoring-plugin\n\nThis rule set applies whenever the `agentic-monitoring-plugin` is active in an Antigravity session.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Stream Surveillance & Safety\n- **Non-Destructive Observation**: Monitoring workers must strictly perform read/tail operations on target streams. Do not kill or alter target processes unless explicitly requested by the user during a pause gate.\n- **Buffer & Resource Management**: Guard against unbound in-memory buffering when tailing high-throughput log streams. Process logs in bounded windows.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Interactive Pause Gate Protocol\n- When an anomaly or error is detected:\n  1. Halt surveillance immediately to prevent log cascading.\n  2. Synthesize an actionable `MonitoringAlert` capturing the root error and stack trace.\n  3. Prompt the user with specific, actionable remediation choices via `ask_question`.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Communication & Links\n- Keep alert summaries high-signal and concise.\n- Always provide clickable markdown links using the `file://` scheme when referencing local log files, configuration paths, or source files implicated in stack traces.\n",
  "Overwrite": true,
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/rules/AGENTS.md"
}
> ```

  "Content": "# agentic-monitoring-plugin\n\nTelemetry, stream surveillance, and log monitoring companion plugin for [Google Antigravity](https://antigravity.google).\n\n## Overview\n`agentic-monitoring-plugin` provides dedicated subagents and progressive-disclosure skills to watch application logs, Docker containers, and serial ports (`/dev/tty*`) continuously in the background. It intercepts errors and crashes early with interactive pause gates.\n\n## Features\n- **Declarative Subagent (`@monitoring`)**: Background streaming worker defined in [`agents/monitoring/agent.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/agents/monitoring/agent.md).\n- **Progressive Skill (`monitoring`)**: Surveillance runbook and trigger instructions in [`skills/monitoring/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/skills/monitoring/SKILL.md).\n- **Interactive Anomaly Gate**: Immediately halts log cascading and queries the user via Antigravity's `ask_question` tool.\n- **Rules (`rules/AGENTS.md`)**: Enforces safe log streaming, bounded buffers, and clean formatting.\n\n## Installation & Activation\n\n```bash\n# Enable in Antigravity session:\nagy plugin enable agentic-monitoring-plugin\n```\n\n## Contributing\nSee [CONTRIBUTING.md](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/CONTRIBUTING.md) for contribution guidelines and testing practices.\n\n## License\nThis project is licensed under the [MIT License](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/LICENSE).\n",

Any secondary updates must be executed *before* calling `write_file(RequestFeedback=true)`, or deferred until *after* the user provides feedback.\",\n        \"     * Immediately stop calling tools after creating the artifact to end your turn so Antigravity presents the interactive review buttons to the user.\",\n        \"   - **Handling User Decisions**: When the user clicks the UI button, Antigravity injects:\",\n        \"     * `[Approved] <artifact>.md`: Proceed immediately to the next stage (`coding-plan` after `specification.md`, `coding-develop` workers after `plan.md`, or ask correction confirmation after `issue-assessment.md`).\",\n        \"     * `[Rejected] <artifact>.md` (with optional feedback comments): Route feedback back to `coding-clarify` or `coding-investigate` to adjust scope and re-synthesize.\",\n        \"5. **Standalone Subagents**:\",\n        \"   - `@coding-investigate`: Standalone issue investigator diagnosing root causes across repositories, formulating solutions in `issue-assessment.md`, requesting user approval, confirming whether to start correction, and invoking `@coding` upon approval.\",\n        \"   - Telemetry and stream monitoring is handled by `@monitoring` via the companion `agentic-monitoring-plugin`.\",",

It bundles a dedicated declarative markdown subagent ([`@monitoring`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/agents/monitoring/agent.md)), a progressive-disclosure monitoring skill ([`skills/monitoring/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/skills/monitoring/SKILL.md)), and an interactive pause gate protocol that halts log cascades upon anomaly detection to prompt the user with remediation choices.\n\n---\n\n## Components\n\n### 1. Manifest, Metadata & Rules\n- **Manifest**: [`plugin.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/plugin.json) declaring name `agentic-monitoring-plugin`, version `0.1.0`, license `MIT`.\n- **Environment**: Managed via `uv` with `pyproject.toml` dependencies (`pydantic>=2.10.0`, `pytest>=8.0.0`).\n- **Rules**: [`rules/AGENTS.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/rules/AGENTS.md) enforcing non-destructive stream observation, bounded log buffers, and interactive anomaly pause gate protocols.\n\n### 2. Declarative Markdown Subagents (`agents/`)\nSubagents are organized in dedicated directories with frontmatter metadata:\n\n| Agent Directory / File | Handle / Name | Role & Capabilities | Tool Permissions |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| [`agents/agent.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/agents/agent.md) | `@agent` | **Monitoring Subagents Overview**: Telemetry architecture, subagent catalog, and anomaly pause gate protocols. | `write=True`, `subagent=False`, `mcp=True` |\n| [`agents/monitoring/agent.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/agents/monitoring/agent.md) | `@monitoring` | **Telemetry Monitor**: Continuous surveillance of application logs, Docker stdout, and serial ports (`/dev/tty*`) with anomaly pause gates. | `write=True`, `subagent=False`, `mcp=True` |\n\n### 3. Progressive-Disclosure Skills (`skills/`)\n| Skill Name | Path | Description | Invocation |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| `monitoring` | [`skills/monitoring/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-monitoring-plugin/skills/monitoring/SKILL.md) | Runbooks and procedures for streaming logs, Docker containers, and serial ports with automatic anomaly detection. | Progressive disclosure / `@monitoring` prompt |\n\n---\n\n## Telemetry & Anomaly Pause Gate Protocol\n\n```text\nStream Target (Log / Container / Serial)\n         │\n         ▼\nContinuous Surveillance Scan (Chunk by chunk)\n         │\n    Anomaly Detected?\n   ├── (No) ──> Continue streaming\n   └── (Yes) ──> Immediately PAUSE surveillance\n                      │\n                      ▼\n            Synthesize MonitoringAlert JSON\n                      │\n                      ▼\n         Prompt User via ask_question\n         - Inspect code around stack trace\n         - Restart process / container\n         - Ignore and continue\n         - Abort monitoring session\n```\n\n---\n\n## Installation & Usage\n\n```bash\n# Enable in Antigravity session:\nagy plugin enable agentic-monitoring-plugin\n\n# Invoke monitoring directly:\n@monitoring watch server logs at /var/log/app.log and alert on 5xx errors\n```\n",

{
  "Content": "---\nname: investigate\ndescription: Standalone issue investigation worker diagnosing root causes across repositories, formulating solutions, and initiating remediation.\n---\n\n# Issue Investigator (`@investigate`)\n\nYou are **investigate**, a standalone issue investigation subagent.\n\n## Mission\nYour mission is to thoroughly investigate software issues, defects, test failures, and unexpected behaviors across all repositories in the workspace, identify their root cause, propose actionable solutions, and coordinate with the user and the coding workflow orchestrator.\n\n## Core Responsibilities & Workflow\n\n### 1. Multi-Repository Root Cause Investigation\n- Thoroughly search for the possible cause of the issue across all repositories in the workspace.\n- Search source code, error messages, stack traces, configs, dependencies, and git history using available search and file viewing tools.\n- Trace data flows, state changes, and cross-repository contracts or API boundaries to locate where the fault originates.\n\n### 2. Solution Formulation\n- Propose one or more concrete, technically sound solutions for the issue.\n- Detail the pros, cons, and architectural impact of each proposed solution.\n\n### 3. User Interrogation & Clarification (when no solution found)\n- If no solution can be found after initial investigation:\n  - Interrogate the user to obtain more context, reproduction steps, logs, environment details, or recent changes.\n  - Ask questions **one at a time** using the `ask_question` tool.\n  - You may ask multiple-choice questions with recommendations or open-ended questions when appropriate.\n  - Continue investigating and grilling until at least one viable solution is found, OR until you identify and confirm that there is no possible solution at the moment.\n  - If no solution is currently possible, clearly report your findings, constraints, and why remediation cannot proceed.\n\n### 4. Issue Assessment Artifact Creation & Approval Gate\n- When one or more solutions are found, produce an **Issue Assessment** artifact (`issue-assessment.md`).\n- **Location**: Write the artifact directly to the session artifact directory:\n  `<appDataDir>/brain/<conversation-id>/issue-assessment.md`.\n- **Content Structure**:\n  - `# Issue Assessment: <Issue Title>`\n  - `## 1. Summary & Observed Behavior`: Overview of the reported issue and symptoms.\n  - `## 2. Root Cause Analysis`: Detailed findings, evidence, affected files and repositories (with clickable links).\n  - `## 3. Proposed Solutions`: Detailed solution options, trade-offs, and recommended approach.\n  - `## 4. Verification & Validation Plan`: How the correction will be verified and tested.\n- **Antigravity Interactive Artifact Approval Protocol**:\n  - You MUST write the artifact using `write_file` with `ArtifactMetadata={\"RequestFeedback\": True, \"UserFacing\": True}`.\n  - **Turn Termination & Sole Tool Call Rule**: `write_file(..., RequestFeedback=True)` MUST be the **sole and final tool call of that turn**. Do not invoke any other tools after it. End the turn immediately so the Antigravity interactive Approve / Reject buttons appear for the user.\n  - Await user approval:\n    - If `[Approved] issue-assessment.md`: Proceed to Step 5 (Correction Confirmation).\n    - If `[Rejected] issue-assessment.md`: Review user feedback, revise the investigation or solutions, update `issue-assessment.md`, and re-request feedback.\n\n### 5. Correction Confirmation Gate\n- Once the Issue Assessment is approved, ask the user if they want to start working on the correction:\n  - Use the `ask_question` tool with a binary Yes/No question:\n    ```python\n    ask_question(\n        questions=[{\n            \"question\": \"Do you want to start working on the correction for this issue?\",\n            \"options\": [\n                \"(Recommended) Yes, start working on the correction\",\n                \"No, do not start working on the correction\"\n            ],\n            \"is_multi_select\": False\n        }],\n        toolAction=\"Confirming correction start\",\n        toolSummary=\"Correction confirmation\"\n    )\n    ```\n- **If the user answers Yes**:\n  - Invoke the `@coding` agent using `invoke_subagent` with a request to implement the correction and a reference to the approved Issue Assessment:\n    ```python\n    invoke_subagent(\n        Subagents=[{\n            \"TypeName\": \"coding\",\n            \"Role\": \"Coding Workflow Orchestrator\",\n            \"Prompt\": \"Implement the correction for the issue based on the approved Issue Assessment artifact at <appDataDir>/brain/<conversation-id>/issue-assessment.md.\",\n            \"Model\": \"inherit\"\n        }],\n        toolAction=\"Invoking coding orchestrator\",\n        toolSummary=\"Start issue remediation\"\n    )\n    ```\n- **If the user answers No**:\n  - Stop there. Do not make code changes and do not invoke any subagents. Inform the user that the investigation is complete and the assessment is saved.\n",

  "Content": "---\nname: agent\ndescription: Architecture overview and catalog for the agentic-investigate-plugin subagents.\n---\n\n# Issue Investigation Subagents & Architecture (`agents/agent.md`)\n\nThis document defines the subagent architecture and catalog for the **`agentic-investigate-plugin`**.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Architectural Overview\n\nThe `agentic-investigate-plugin` provides autonomous root cause analysis, user interrogation, solution formulation, and remediation handoffs across multi-repository workspaces. It isolates bug hunting and defect diagnostics into a specialized subagent (`@investigate`), ensuring code is never modified without evidence-backed root cause analysis and explicit human approval.\n\n### Directory Structure\n```text\nagents/\n├── agent.md                    # Master subagent guide (this file)\n└── investigate/                # Standalone Issue Investigator (@investigate)\n    └── agent.md\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Subagent Catalog\n\n| Agent Handle | Directory | `enable_write_tools` | `enable_subagent_tools` | `enable_mcp_tools` | Description |\n| :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |\n| **`@investigate`** | [`agents/investigate/`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/agents/investigate/) | `True` | `True` | `True` | Standalone multi-repository root cause investigator producing `issue-assessment.md` artifacts, grilling users when needed, and handing off approved fixes to `@coding`. |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Investigation & Remediation Protocol\n\n```text\nMulti-Repo Search ──> Solution Found? ──(No)──> Grill User (ask_question) ──┐\n                            │                                                │\n                          (Yes) <────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n                Write issue-assessment.md (RequestFeedback=True)\n                            │\n                     [Approved by User]\n                            ▼\n            Ask Correction Confirmation (Yes / No)\n                     │                    │\n                   (Yes)                 (No)\n                     ▼                    ▼\n              Invoke @coding            Stop\n```\n\n### Mandatory Artifact Approval Gate\n- **Location**: `<appDataDir>/brain/<conversation-id>/issue-assessment.md`\n- **Metadata**: `ArtifactMetadata={\"RequestFeedback\": True, \"UserFacing\": True}`\n- **Sole & Final Tool Call Rule**: `write_file` must be the sole and final tool call of that turn to trigger the interactive Approve / Reject buttons in Antigravity.\n",

  "Content": "---\nname: investigate\ndescription: >-\n  Standalone worker subagent for multi-repository issue investigation, root cause diagnosis,\n  solution proposal, interactive issue assessment approval, and remediation handoff.\n---\n\n# Issue Investigator (`@investigate`)\n\nThe `investigate` subagent investigates complex bugs, anomalies, test failures, and unexpected behaviors across repositories.\n\n## Workflow\n\n1. **Multi-Repo Root Cause Search**:\n   - Searches code, logs, stack traces, configs, and git history across all repositories in the workspace to pinpoint the root cause.\n2. **Propose Solutions**:\n   - Outlines one or more viable solutions with pros, cons, and architectural impact.\n3. **User Clarification / Grilling**:\n   - If no solution can be determined, interrogates the user via `ask_question` (asking one question at a time, open-ended or choice) until a solution is found or it determines that no solution is possible.\n4. **Issue Assessment Artifact Gate**:\n   - Writes `issue-assessment.md` directly to the session artifact directory with `RequestFeedback=True` and `UserFacing=True` as the sole and final tool call of the turn.\n   - Waits for user approval (`[Approved]` or `[Rejected]`).\n5. **Correction Confirmation & Handoff**:\n   - Once approved, asks the user via `ask_question` whether to start working on the correction (Yes/No).\n   - If Yes: invokes `@coding` (`invoke_subagent(TypeName=\"coding\", Role=\"Coding Workflow Orchestrator\", Prompt=...)`) with a reference to the issue assessment.\n   - If No: halts without modifying code.\n\n## Triggering the Subagent\n\n- Type `@investigate Investigate why payments time out in checkout`\n- Or prompt: \"Please investigate the cause of the 500 error in auth service\"\n",

  "Content": "# Issue Investigation Standards — agentic-investigate-plugin\n\nThis rule set applies whenever the `agentic-investigate-plugin` is active in an Antigravity session.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Investigation Principles\n- **Evidence-Based Diagnosis**: Never speculate or propose code modifications without tangible evidence (stack traces, logs, git commits, reproducible steps).\n- **Multi-Repository Discipline**: Trace cross-service RPCs, REST calls, shared libraries, and schema contracts across all repositories before concluding root causes.\n- **Read-Only Investigation**: During the diagnostic phase, do not alter application source code or tests. Modifying code occurs only after human approval through the coding orchestrator (`@coding`).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Interactive User Interrogation & Approval Gates\n- **One Question at a Time**: When asking for user clarification, always use `ask_question` asking one question at a time.\n- **Sole and Final Tool Call Rule**:\n  - Writing `issue-assessment.md` with `RequestFeedback=True` must be the sole and final tool call of that turn.\n  - Never batch secondary tool calls after authoring the artifact so Antigravity renders the interactive Approve / Reject buttons.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Communication & Links\n- Keep reports structured, concise, and high-signal.\n- Always generate clickable markdown links using the `file://` scheme when referencing affected files, functions, and configuration files.\n",
  "Overwrite": true,
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/rules/AGENTS.md"
}

It bundles a dedicated declarative markdown subagent ([`@investigate`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/agents/investigate/agent.md)), a progressive-disclosure investigation skill ([`skills/investigate/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/skills/investigate/SKILL.md)), user clarification protocols, an interactive issue assessment approval gate (`issue-assessment.md`), and a remediation confirmation handoff to `@coding`.\n\n---\n\n## Components\n\n### 1. Manifest, Metadata & Rules\n- **Manifest**: [`plugin.json`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/plugin.json) declaring name `agentic-investigate-plugin`, version `0.1.0`, license `MIT`.\n- **Environment**: Managed via `uv` with `pyproject.toml` dependencies (`pydantic>=2.10.0`, `pytest>=8.0.0`).\n- **Rules**: [`rules/AGENTS.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/rules/AGENTS.md) enforcing evidence-based diagnosis, multi-repository search discipline, read-only diagnostic safety, and interactive artifact approval protocols.\n\n### 2. Declarative Markdown Subagents (`agents/`)\nSubagents are organized in dedicated directories with frontmatter metadata:\n\n| Agent Directory / File | Handle / Name | Role & Capabilities | Tool Permissions |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| [`agents/agent.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/agents/agent.md) | `@agent` | **Investigation Subagents Overview**: Investigation architecture, subagent catalog, and remediation protocols. | `write=True`, `subagent=True`, `mcp=True` |\n| [`agents/investigate/agent.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/agents/investigate/agent.md) | `@investigate` | **Issue Investigator**: Autonomous multi-repository root cause investigator producing `issue-assessment.md` artifacts, grilling users when needed, and handing off approved fixes to `@coding`. | `write=True`, `subagent=True`, `mcp=True` |\n\n### 3. Progressive-Disclosure Skills (`skills/`)\n| Skill Name | Path | Description | Invocation |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| `investigate` | [`skills/investigate/SKILL.md`](file:///Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-investigate-plugin/skills/investigate/SKILL.md) | Runbooks and diagnostic procedures for tracing cross-repository bugs, user grilling, and issue assessment authoring. | Progressive disclosure / `@investigate` prompt |\n\n---\n\n## Investigation & Remediation Workflow\n\n```text\nMulti-Repo Search ──> Solution Found? ──(No)──> Grill User (ask_question) ──┐\n                            │                                                │\n                          (Yes) <────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n                Write issue-assessment.md (RequestFeedback=True)\n                            │\n                     [Approved by User]\n                            ▼\n            Ask Correction Confirmation (Yes / No)\n                     │                    │\n                   (Yes)                 (No)\n                     ▼                    ▼\n              Invoke @coding            Stop\n```\n\n### Interactive Artifact Approval Protocol\n1. **Artifact Location**: `<appDataDir>/brain/<conversation-id>/issue-assessment.md`.\n2. **Metadata**: `ArtifactMetadata={\"RequestFeedback\": True, \"UserFacing\": True}`.\n3. **Sole & Final Tool Call Rule**: `write_file(..., RequestFeedback=True)` MUST be the sole and final tool call of that turn so Antigravity renders the interactive **Approve / Reject** buttons.\n4. **Correction Confirmation Gate**: Once approved, prompts the user via `ask_question` whether to start the correction. If confirmed, invokes `@coding` (`invoke_subagent`).\n\n---\n\n## Installation & Usage\n\n```bash\n# Enable in Antigravity session:\nagy plugin enable agentic-investigate-plugin\n\n# Invoke investigation directly:\n@investigate Investigate why payments time out in checkout\n```\n",
  "Overwrite": true,
  "Path": "/Users/gpfister/Development/Projects/gpfister/agentic/agentic-obsidian/plugins/agentic-investigate-plugin.md"
}
> ```
